The list is not by any means complete.
as very many enumerators have failed to separate such bodies of population.
l'he population of these places has, of course. been includer
in the townships in which they are respectively situated.
The figures can be considered as only approximate, as the limits of such places
are not sharply delined.

NOTE.—The following unincorporated places have been returned separately by the enumerators.
The hst is not ky any means complete
as very many enumerators have failed to separate such bodies of population.
The population of these places has, of course, been ineluded
in the precincts in which they are respectively situated. The figures can be considered as only approximate, as the limits of such places
are not sharply defined.

Note.-The following unincorporated places have been returned separately by the enumerators.

complote, as very many enumerators have failed to separate
such bodies of popnlation.
The population of these places has, of course,
beon included in the magistorial districts in which they are respectively situated.
The figures can be considered as only approximate;
as the limits of such places are not sharply defined.
